Can we exploit global optimum structure by solving KKT linear with dense weights via FFT fixed-point? Let's perhaps implement an "alternating active weights linear solver" as a new method:
```
def kkt_fixed_point(a, max_iter, beta):
  a normalized
  for it:
    c, M = ...
    w = softmax(beta*(c-M))
    g = corr_pair(a, w)  # linear gradient (one-sided sum w a)
    # condition g = const on support
    step = adaptive
    a = normalize(clip(a * exp(-alpha*(g - weighted_mean?)/scale),0))
```
But as discussed, log gradient should be α * (g - const)? Let's derive update for minimizing exact F with active weight w. F_w = 2n (∑w c)/S^2. Since w fixed, we can optimize this surrogate. A more effective step for q_w/S^2 is multiplicative from log gradient: v_i -= step * a_i (g_i/S? - ...). With S=1, gradient = 4n a_i (g_i - C_w?)? Because dF = (4n C_w^2/S^3?) This update keeps nonnegative and preserves scale-ish. Let's implement:
```
g = corr_pair(a,w)  # one-sided
mu = np.dot(a, g) / S  # weighted mean? KKT: C/S =? C=∑w c =? ∑_i a_i g_i? Check identity: C = ∑_k w c_k = ∑_k w∑_i a_i a_{k-i} = ∑_i a_i ∑_k w a_{k-i} = ∑ a_i g_i. So μ=C/S = dot(a,g)/S. Good.
delta = (g - mu)/np.max(np.abs(g-mu))
a *= exp(-alpha * a * delta * n?)  # need scale
```
Since a_i ~1/n, g~1/n, delta~O(1/n). a_i*delta ~O(1/n^2); need multiply by n or S? To get O(1) exponent, use step_scale*S? Let's nondimensionalize d = n c/S^2. Let g_d = n g/S^2? Then μ_d = nC/S^3? Pseudo for KKT optimizer:
```
def kkt_optimize(start, deadline, label):
    a=normalize(start)
    best=a.copy(); best_s=exact_score(a)
    n=len(a)
    beta_list=(1,3,5,10,30,100,300,1000,3000,10000,100000,1e6?)
    k_list=(1,2,3,5,8,13,21,34)
    eta_lin=(0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1,2,5)
    eta_mult=(0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1)
    count=0
    while time<deadline:
       d,S=d_from_a(a)
       dmax=d.max()
       improved=False
       # active weights candidates list capped by time
       weights_list=[]
       for k in small: weights from topk
       for beta in selected: w=softmax; if not too close to previous?
       for each w:
          g=weighted_corr
          target=np.dot(a,g)/S
          val=g-target
          # possibly center val so sum weighted zero: val -= np.dot(a,val)/S? Not necessary; normalization will adjust. But to preserve sum before normalize, subtract weighted mean maybe: val -= np.dot(a,val)/S to make ∑a val=0. This is like constraint. Could use.
          for eta:
             q=a - eta*val
             q=np.maximum(q,0); q=normalize(q); sc=exact; update best and a if sc<a_current
          # multiplicative
             rel=val/a_safe
             q=a*np.exp(-eta*clip(rel,-3,3)); q=normalize; sc; update
       if not improved for several loop break
       # occasionally add small noise to avoid local: if stagnation, perturb a
```
Need avoid evaluating too many. At each outer, maybe 8 weights * (5 linear+4 mult)=72 exact scores. If 500 iters=36k scores; okay maybe 20s? plus corr.

But we can be more selective: Use best previous weights that improved; adapt. Simpler: For each weight, try etas and if improvement update; after all, if no improvement break.

Line search for val: The objective as function of eta along q(eta)=normalize(a - η val) may be convex for small; we can use a few steps and pick best. We don't need exact line search every time; try geometric. Use `current_s` to avoid re-evaluating a. For each weight, compute candidates; select best candidate; if best <= current, set a=best; update global. This reduces exact calls: evaluate candidates, score best. Still 10 per weight.

Could do backtracking with e.g. initial eta based on step size: `eta0 = 0.1 * n / (n*maxabs?)` since val magnitude O(1/n). We can just use fixed.

Multiplicative update might be unstable if val/a large. Clip rel to [-2,2] (or [-5,5]). For eta=1, factor exp(-2)=0.135/7.4. Use clip.

We should also try active weight from **lower** lags? Since minimizing max, active are high d. Maybe top-k. Good.

What about using anti-active weights to explore? Could use weights from lower c? Sign flip? Since nonconvex, but accepting improvements only safe. We can include `sign=+1` (increase where val high) with multiplicative? It may escape bad local but rarely improve; maybe include rare.

Important: The objective is maximum of c. The active set at current may be a single lag. To flatten, we need weight on multiple near-max lags. The softmax beta small gives broad weight, which can be effective. We should include beta near 0? As beta→0, w uniform over all 2n-1 lags. Then g=average over all k a_{k-i}; target; val may push to reduce edge/central imbalance. Uniform active (all lags) corresponds making convolution as flat as possible in L1 sense. Good. For top-k, top1 may not improve. Top2/3 near peak.

Could also use **edge-correcting weights**: weights on lowest lags? The max is high; but to maintain flat, maybe need to increase low lags? Since objective only max, we don't care low, but balancing by shifting mass may lower high. The KKT weights are nonnegative on active high lags only. Low lags not active. So okay.

Let's think of KKT fixed point more: If active weights w chosen, solving H a=target 1 may produce a. Our linear update approximates. Could accelerate by using Aitken? For a fixed w, H a = target? target depends on a (C/S), so nonlinear but under normalization. We can view minimizing q_w = C/S^2; this is a quadratic ratio. The optimum of q_w over simplex can be found by generalized eigenvalue? q_w = a^T H a? Actually C=∑w c = a^T P a (with P symmetric? positive part). q=C/S^2. Minimize over simplex. This is a quadratic form ratio with linear constraint. If P is PSD, solution generalized eigenvector. If not, nonconvex. But for a fixed w, we can do power iteration? The minimum of Rayleigh quotient over simplex? If allow scaling, min over a of a^T P a / (∑a)^2. This is not standard Rayleigh (denom linear^2). Let b=a/S (simplex). q=b^T H b? Actually C(b)=b^T P b, S=1, minimize b^T P b over b≥0, sum1. This is quadratic programming, nonconvex if P indefinite. But can solve via active set? The KKT for fixed w: P b = μ 1 on support (P symmetric maybe). Here P = (R+R^T)/2 weighted. Gradient 2P b; condition P b= μ 1. That's matrix-vector equation (not H a = target because target C/S = a^T P? For q=C/S, derivative =2(P b - C 1); zero P b = C 1). C = b^T P b = sum b_i (P b)_i. So val = P b - C 1. Our g-target should correspond to P b - C1. Good. We can solve P b = C1, where C=b^T P b. This is nonlinear but fixed point. If P PSD, minimum maybe eigenvector? For a constant vector 1? Since sum constraint, at interior stationary P b = C 1. This means b is in range? If P has all-ones eigen? If P 1 = rho 1 then b uniform. For weighted convolution P, row sums vary, so nonuniform.

Could use **Newton for fixed w**: P b = b^T P b 1. Since P b =? C = b·u where u=P b. So u = (b·u) 1. This is an equation. Not easy.

But linear Richardson b <- b - η(Pb - C1) is a method to minimize quadratic b^T P b (if P PSD) under sum? It moves orthogonal to 1? We can project: progress direction d=Pb - C1. Note ∑ b d = b·Pb - C∑b =0. So linear update preserves sum exactly! Nice. It also changes coefficients. This is gradient of C under sum constraint. Good. We can use η. The ideal η for quadratic if P has condition κ is 2/(λmax+λmin). We can estimate λmax by Rayleigh quotient on direction? But can just try.

Since d has weighted sum zero, normalization after clip not necessary if no negatives. If negatives, project.

This suggests a `quad_optimize_fixed_w`: For fixed w, iterate b = b - η(Pb - C1). The objective q_w decreases if P positive-like. We can run many iterations with fixed w, then update w. This is like projected gradient. We can estimate optimal η by backtracking on q_w (not exact max). Could be faster.

Let's implement in KKT optimizer: For selected w, run a mini-line search sequence of η maybe; update a; this is same as linear update with exact score. Good.

Can we compute P b directly with `corr`? P b_i = ∑w a_{k-i} (one-sided) = g. C=dot(a,g). Good.

Maybe use **over-relaxation**: `a_new = a + ρ*( -g + C1 )`. If η too large, may oscillate/negative. We can try η up to maybe 20? Since d O(1/n). For n=10k, a~1e-4, d~1e-4, η=10 gives change 1e-3 relative 10, might negative. Normalization & clip handles. But too high may destroy. We can use adaptive: If objective improved with last η, maybe increase; else decrease.

```

s=a.sum()

```

```

```

```

```
def lse_score_grad(a, beta):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    S=a.sum(); if S<... return 1e8, zeros
    n=len(a)
    d,S=d_from_a(a) # S maybe same
    dmax=d.max()
    if dmax<=0: ...
    z=beta*(d-dmax); z=clip(z,-50,50)
    p=exp(z); psum=p.sum(); if psum<=0 p= onehot
    p/=psum
    # L = dmax + np.log(psum)/beta if not clipped? With clipped, logsumexp not exact. Use stable without clipping:
       # maxz= zmax=0 (since z<=0); logsumexp = maxz + logsum(exp(z - maxz)) = logsum(exp(z)). z maybe very negative, exp underflow to0 but psum positive from max. min z can be -inf due clip -50. Use clip at -50 okay for tail.
    L=dmax + np.log(psum)/beta
    # score=2L + PEN*(S-1)^2
    C_p = np.dot(a,g?) Need g. Use corr.
    # g = weighted_corr(p)
    g = corr_from_weights(p...)
    C_p = np.dot(a,g)/S
    d_p = n*C_p/(S*S)? since d_w = dot(p,d) = nC/S^2.
    # gradient of 2L + penalty:
    grad = (4*n/(S**3))*(S*g - C_p)  # from earlier for F=2L? Let's verify: ∂(2L)/∂a = (4n/S^3)(S g - C_p). For S=1:4n(g-C). Good.
    grad += 2*PEN*(S-1)*np.ones(n)
    # If using LSE with clipped z, gradient tail wrong but okay.
    return 2L+PEN*(S-1)^2, grad
```
But is C_p=dot(a,g)/S correct? C_p=dot(a,g) (unnormalized) because C_w=∑w∑a_i a_{k-i}=∑a_i g_i. Yes. For gradient of C/S^2, ∂ =2g/S^2 -2C/S^3. times 2L? Wait F=2L. Derivative of L: p_k ∂d_k. ∂d= n/S^3 (S g - C)*2? We got grad L = (2n/S^3)(S g - C). F=2L => (4n/S^3)(S g -C). correct.

However LSE of d uses d=n c/S^2; p sum1. If S not 1, d computed normalized. Need g to correspond ∑p a_{k-i}. C_p=∑a_i g_i. Grad formula uses S. Good.

Penalty: PEN=1e-5? If S=1 kept. Previous 1e-3. Use small 1e-6 to avoid bias but L-BFGS may drift. Since objective scale invariant plus penalty, optimum has S close 1. Good. We can set PEN=1e-8? KKT optimizer details and adaptive weights:
```
def kkt_optimize(a_start, deadline, label, max_stagnation=30):
    a=normalize(a_start); best=a.copy(); best_s=exact_score(a); current_s=best_s
    n=len(a)
    beta_choices=(0.0, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0, 100.0, 300.0, 1000.0, 3000.0, 10000.0)
    k_choices=(1,2,3,4,6,9,14,22,36)
    eta_lin=(0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0,8.0)
    eta_mult=(0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.5)
    stagn=0
    iter=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       d,_=d_from_a(a)
       improved=False
       # Prepare weight candidates: top-k and softmax. Limit number based on time? We can sample.
       cands=[]
       for k in k_choices: cands.append(('k',k,None))
       for beta in beta_choices: cands.append(('b',None,beta))
       # To save time, if many, sort by last usefulness? Simple all.
       best_candidate=(a,current_s)
       for mode,k,beta in cands:
          if time>deadline: break
          # compute weights
          if mode=='k':
             idx=topk_indices(d,k); weights=full(1/k); g=direct_corr
          else:
             if beta==0: w=uniform; g=corr_dense
             else: p,idx,wts=softmax; if len(idx)<=64: g=direct else g=corr_dense(p)
          C=np.dot(a,g)
          val=g - C  # since S=1 (we keep normalized)
          # To keep high-level sum, optionally center: val -= np.dot(a,val) (should be zero analytically if g from same? ∑a_i g_i = C; ∑a_i (g-C)=C-C=0. yes)
          maxv=max(abs(val))
          if maxv<1e-15: continue
          # linear updates
          for eta in eta_lin:
              q=a - eta*val
              # if eta too large negative, clip
              if q.min()<0: q=np.clip(q,0,None)
              q=normalize(q)
              s=exact_score(q)
              if finite and s<best_s: best...
              if finite and s<current_s: current best candidate update? We can update a to best within this weight to continue.
          # multiplicative
          a_safe=np.maximum(a,1e-300)
          rel=val/a_safe
          # clip
          for eta in eta_mult:
              q=a*np.exp(-eta*np.clip(rel,-4,4))
              q=normalize(q)
              s=exact_score(q); update
       if improved: stagn=0 else stagn+=1
       if stagn>20:
          # perturb a from best with small noise
          a=perturb(best, rng, small)
          current_s=exact_score(a)
       iter++
```
But this loop evaluates many candidates each iter; best_candidate approach: We can simply update `a` whenever an improvement found (current), and global best. At end of weight list, if no improvement for many, perturb.

To reduce scoring, for each weight, compute a set of q and score, choose the best q for that weight; if better than current, set a=best_q. This requires same number scores (len etas) but allows using best. We'll do:
```
base_s=current_s
best_q=a; best_qs=base_s
for eta: score q; if s<best_qs: best_qs=s; best_q=q
if best_qs<base_s: a=best_q; current_s=best_qs; improved=True; if <global update
```
For multiplicative, either separately. Could choose step less. This means after linear, use improved a for mult? Maybe use original a for each direction to compare. Use original `a0=a` within weight; find best linear and best mult; select best overall and update once per weight. Good.

We can limit number of exponentials by time: If SUFFICIENT, maybe do all. 27 candidates * maybe 30 iters? okay.

Adaptive eta: Could store last best eta for each weight type to reduce. But simpler.

Potential issue: `exact_score` every q includes FFT. 9 k +10 beta =19 weights. Linear 6 + mult6 =12 candidates each =>228 exact per full iteration. If 20 iterations =>4560 exact. Each exact d_from_a FFT N=32768. 4560*0.3ms=1.4s? Actually Python overhead maybe 5ms =>23s. Corr dense adds. Fine. If 100 iterations =>200s. Good.

We can use exact score of q via `d_from_a`; no need verify with evaluate_sequence.

But KKT direction `val` computed from current `d`; after updating a within weight, we don't recompute d until next outer. Good.

We may want to use **active weights from d normalized**, not c. d = n c/S^2; top same. For softmax beta in d. Since dmax ~0.75, beta values in d. Direct.

Uniform weight over all lags: corr_dense with w length 2n-1. g_i = average of all valid a_j with weight 1/(2n-1). C=dot(a,g). This direction tries to flatten overall P. Good.

Top-k for k> maybe top 36. Direct 36 slices per corr; each slice length ~n; 36*10k=360k ops per weight. 9 top weights =>3.2M ops per outer; okay. Use direct.

```
def lowdim_search(n, deadline, rng):
   x=...
   # basis using Fourier sine/cosine odd/even plus polynomial
   B=np.column_stack([...]) # n x D
   # define func(theta):
      v=B@theta
      v-=v.mean()
      a=np.exp(v-v.max())
      a/=a.sum()
      return exact_score(a), a
   # grid initial: zeros, and random sorted by exact score.

Let's design `fun_lseb` top-level:
```
def fun_lseb(x, beta):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, ...)
    x = np.maximum(x,0.0)
    S = float(x.sum())
    if S < 1e-12: return 1e10, zeros
    n = len(x)
    c = fast_self_conv(x)
    d = n*c/(S*S)
    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    if not finite or dmax<=0: ...
    z = float(beta)*(d-dmax)
    # stable logsumexp without clipping too much
    # Since z<=0, max 0. Use np.exp(np.minimum(z,0))? Actually z maximum 0. For large negative underflow.
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0))  # underflow tail ignored
    ps = e.sum()
    if ps <=0: e[d.argmax()]=1; ps=1
    e /= ps  # p
    L = dmax + np.log(ps)/float(beta) if beta>1e-8 else dmax + (e? no)
    # But if clipped at -50, ps underestimates. Could use unclipped with `np.exp(z)` since z <=0 and min -large; underflow to0 okay. No need clip except for inf. Use z = np.where(z < -50, -50, z) for safety.
    # if beta=0? not use.
    # weight p
    if float(beta) < 1e-8: p = ones(len)/len
    else: p=e/ps
    # choose corr
    pidx maybe for direct if sparse
    nnz = int(np.count_nonzero(p > 1e-12))
    if nnz <= 64 and nnz < len(p): idx=np.nonzero(p>1e-12)[0]; pw=p[idx]/p[idx].sum(); g=direct
    else: g=corr_dense
    C = np.dot(x,g)  # not divided S? x sum S. C = sum x g.
    # gradient formula:
    grad = (4.0 * n /(S**3)) * (S*g - C)
    # penalty
    grad += 2.0 * PEN_SUM * (S-1.0)
    score = 2.0*L + PEN_SUM*(S-1.0)**2
    # gradient scale maybe; nan_to_num
    return score, grad
```

Pseudo:
```
def kkt_descent(a0, deadline, rng, label):
    a=normalize(a0); best=a.copy; best_s=exact_score(a); s=best_s
    no_improve=0
    direction_pool = [('k',1), ('k',2), ('k',3), ('k',5), ('k',8), ('k',13), ('beta',10), ('beta',50), ('beta',200), ('beta',1000), ('beta',5000), ('uniform',None)]
    rng order? We can cycle but include random selection.
    while time<deadline:
       d,_=d_from_a(a)
       improved=False
       # choose order: if no_improve small, cycle all; if large, random sample
       for mode,param in direction_pool:
          if time>deadline: break
          val, g, C = compute_direction(a,d,mode,param)
          if val is None: continue
          maxv=np.max(np.abs(val))
          if maxv < 1e-15: continue
          scale=max(a)/maxv
          # candidate etas linear: scale* (0.05,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0,10.0) maybe cap 100
          best_q=a; best_qs=s
          for f in (0.05,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0,10.0): eta=scale*f
              q=a - eta*val; if any<0 clip; q=normalize; qs=exact; if qs<best_qs: ...
          # mult
          rel=val/np.maximum(a, floor)
          for f in (0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0):
              q=a*np.exp(-f*clip(rel,-3,3))
          if best_qs < s - 1e-12:
             a=best_q; s=best_qs; improved=True
             if s < best_s: best_s=s; best=a.copy()
       if improved: no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve+=1
          if no_improve % 5 ==0:
             # small perturbation
             a=perturb(a, rng, strength=0.05)
             s=exact_score(a)
             if s >= best_s: # don't reset? We can keep perturbed to explore, but if worse too much, reset to best
                if s > best_s*1.02: a=best.copy(); s=best_s
          if no_improve > 30:
             a=best.copy(); s=best_s; no_improve=0
    return best_s,best
```
Need `exact_score` many times; update `s` only when current accepted. But when evaluating candidates for each direction, use candidate score; if no improvement over `s`, discard. Good.

But if a direction's gradient is wrong and all candidates worse than current, we move next. If no direction improves, perturbation. This is a coordinate descent in direction space.

def fit_bcoeffs(a, B):
    y=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); y-=y.mean()
    AtB=B.T@B; rhs=B.T@y
    try theta=np.linalg.solve(AtB,rhs) except theta=zeros
```

    elif mode4: reverse blend
    else: local: select two intervals increase/decrease

`run_lbfgs(a0, beta, deadline, maxfun)`:
```
def run_lbfgs(a0,beta,deadline,maxfun):
   a=normalize_a(a0); n=len(a)
   if deadline-time < 10: return exact_score(a),a
   # smoothing beta; if beta<10 no
   maxfun=max(50, maxfun)
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   opts={'maxiter':max(max(50,maxfun//4),20), 'maxfun':maxfun, 'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12}
   try:
      res=minimize(fun_lseb, a, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(float(beta),), bounds=bounds, options=opts)
   except: return exact_score(a),a
   a2=normalize_a(res.x)
   sc=exact_score(a2)
   if not finite: return exact_score(a),a
   return sc,a2
```
`fun_lseb` as above. For penalty, use PEN_SUM=1e-6? Set global `PEN_SUM=1e-5`.

def estimate_maxfun(left,n):

   est=0.002*max(1.0,n/8192.0)*max(1.0,np.log2(max(16,n))/13)
   return int(min(1000,max(50,0.7*left/est)))
```

Detailed `softmax_weights`:
```
def softmax_weights(d, beta):
    m=d.size
    if beta is None or beta <=0:
       w=np.ones(m)/m
       idx=np.arange(m)
       wt=w[idx]
       return w, idx, wt, True  # dense? uniform but all nonzero
    dmax=float(np.max(d))
    z=float(beta)*(d-dmax)
    # avoid underflow; z <=0
    z=np.where(z < -50.0, -50.0, z)
    e=np.exp(z)
    ps=e.sum()
    if ps<=0 or not finite:
       idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(d))]); wt=np.array([1.0]); w=zeros; w[idx]=1; return w, idx, wt, False
    p=e/ps
    threshold=1e-12
    idx=np.nonzero(p>threshold)[0]
    if idx.size==0: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(d))])
    s=p[idx].sum()
    wt=p[idx]/s
    if idx.size < m//4:
       w=np.zeros(m); w[idx]=wt
    else:
       w=p
    return w, idx, wt, (idx.size <= 80)
```
Return `use_direct` if idx small. For dense corr, use `w` (full). For direct, use idx,wt.
For beta small, idx size large, use direct false, w=p (or e/ps). For beta=0, direct false. But uniform direction zero; we may not call beta=0. If called, direct_corr with m huge impossible. We'll avoid.

`direction_topk(a,d,k)`:

g=direct_corr_topk(a,d,k)
C=np.dot(a,g)
val=g-C
return val,g,C
```
`direct_corr_topk(a,d,k)`:
```
n=len(a); idx=topk_indices(d,k); out=zeros(n); inv=1.0/k
for kk in idx:
   i0=max(0,kk-n+1); i1=min(n-1,kk)
   out[i0:i1+1] += inv * a[kk-i1:kk-i0+1][::-1]
return out
```
For n large and k=1 fast.

`direction_softmax(a,d,beta)`:
```
w,idx,wt,use_direct=softmax_weights(d,beta)
if use_direct: g=direct_corr_weights(a,idx,wt)
else: g=corr_dense(a,w)
C=dot; val=g-C
return val
```

Low-dim Fourier search: We need not overcomplicate. Implement:
```
def fourier_search(n, deadline, rng, parent):
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   p=2*x-1
   B=np.column_stack([p, p**3, np.sin(2πx), np.cos(2πx), np.sin(4πx), np.cos(4πx), np.sin(6πx)])
   # maybe scale columns? PCA to unit norm to avoid uneven.
   B = B / np.linalg.norm(B,axis=0, keepdims=True)
   theta0=...
   starts=[] ...
   best_a=None; best_s=inf
   for theta in starts:
       t_chunk = deadline - (remaining-1)*20?
       try res=minimize(fourier_func, theta, args=(B,), method='Powell', options={'maxiter':300,'xtol':1e-6,'ftol':1e-8})
       except: res.x=theta
       a=make_from_b(res.x,B); sc=exact_score(a); update
       # also evaluate start
   return best_s,best_a
```
But `minimize` may take long; maxiter 300. Powell can call many. For 5 starts, enough. It calls `fourier_func` which calls `exact_score`; exact_score each time computes FFT. Good.
Need `make_from_b` top-level.
```
def fourier_func(theta,B):
   a=make_from_b(theta,B)
   if a is None: return 1e10
   return exact_score(a)
```
`make_from_b` returns normalized array or None.
```
def make_from_b(theta,B):
   v=B.dot(theta)
   v=np.clip(v,-2.5,2.5)
   a=np.exp(v)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return None
   return a/s
```
But if B large, theta large, v clipped; okay.

def lse_path(a_start, deadline):
    a=normalize(a_start); best=a; best_s=exact_score(a)
    betas=(20,100,500,2000,10000,50000,200000)
    for beta in betas:
       while time<deadline:
          left=deadline-time
          mf=estimate_maxfun(left, len(a))
          if mf<50: break
          sc,a2=run_lbfgs(a,beta,left,mf)
          # if sc<best update
          # update current a always to a2 if finite and not awful
          if exact_score(a2) < best_s*1.05: a=normalize(a2) else: a=best.copy()
          if sc>=best_s and beta>2000: break?
    return best_s,best
```

```
def smooth_noise(rng,n,smooth=20):
    z=rng.standard_normal(n)
    # circular lowpass via FFT
    N=next_power_of_two(n)
    F=np.fft.rfft(z,N)
    freqs=np.fft.rfftfreq(N)
    cutoff=smooth / float(N)
    F[:-1] *= (freqs[:-1] < cutoff) maybe smooth not sharp
    out=np.fft.irfft(F,N,n)[:n]
    out-=out.mean()
    std=out.std(); if std>0: out=out/std
    return out
```
Sharp cutoff okay. Or Gaussian kernel. Use FFT.

`next_power_of_two`:
```
def next_power_of_two(m):
    m=int(max(1,m))
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

`fast_self_conv`:
```
def fast_self_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=int(a.size)
    if n==0: return np.empty(0,dtype=np.float64)
    M=2*n-1
    N=next_power_of_two(M)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:M]
    return np.nan_to_num(out,nan=0.0,posinf=0.0,neginf=0.0)
```

`normalize_a`:
```
def normalize_a(a,target=1.0):
    a=np.ndarray? np.asarray...
    if a.size==0: return np.array([],dtype)
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<0.01? Wait for internal during LSE S may be small; if s tiny return uniform. But if a all zero, target/n.
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, target/float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a*(target/s)
```
Use target 1.0. In final, sum 1.

`d_from_a`:
```
def d_from_a(a):
    a=np.asarray...; n=a.size; S=float(a.sum())
    if S<=1e-300: return zeros, S
    c=fast_self_conv(a)
    d=(float(n)*c)/(S*S)
    d=np.maximum(d,0.0)
    return d,S
```

`exact_score`: same.

`direct_corr_weights`:
```
def direct_corr_weights(a, idx, weights):
    a=np.asarray...; n=a.size; out=np.zeros(n)
    if idx is None: return out
    idx=np.asarray(idx); weights=np.asarray(weights)
    for ii in range(idx.size):
       kk=int(idx[ii]); ww=float(weights[ii])
       if ww==0 or kk<0 or kk>=2*n-1: continue
       i0=kk-(n-1)
       if i0<0: i0=0
       i1=kk
       if i1>=n: i1=n-1
       if i0>i1: continue
       out[i0:i1+1] += ww * a[kk-i1:kk-i0+1][::-1]
    return out
```
If weights length mismatch, min.

`direct_corr_topk`:

def direct_corr_topk(a,d,k):
   n=len(a); m=d.size; k=int(min(max(1,k),m))
   if k==1: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(d))])
   elif k>=m: idx=np.arange(m)
   else: idx=np.argpartition(d,m-k)[m-k:]
   out=zeros(n); inv=1.0/k
   for kk in idx: ...
   return out
```

def corr_dense(a,w):
   a; n; m=w.size; M=n+m-1; N=next_power_of_two(M)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N); W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
   full=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)[:M]
   return full[n-1:2*n-1]
```

def corr_dense_pre(a,A,w):
   n=len(a); M=3*n-2
   W=np.fft.rfft(w,len(A))
   full=np.fft.irfft(A*W,len(A))[:M]
   return full[n-1:2*n-1]
```

`softmax_weights`:
```
def softmax_weights(d,beta):
    m=d.size
    if beta <= 1e-12:
       w=np.ones(m)/m; return w
    dmax=float(np.max(d))
    z=float(beta)*(d-dmax)
    z=np.where(z < -50.0, -50.0, z)
    e=np.exp(z)
    ps=float(e.sum())
    if not finite or ps<=0:
       w=np.zeros(m); w[int(np.argmax(d))]=1.0; return w
    p=e/ps
    return p
```
For direction, we can decide sparse by threshold:
```
def weights_to_direct_info(p, threshold=1e-12, max_direct=80):
   idx=np.nonzero(p>threshold)[0]
   if idx.size <= max_direct:
       wt=p[idx]; s=wt.sum(); if s<=0: ...; return idx, wt/s, True
   return None,None,False
```

`smooth_noise` top-level:
```
def smooth_noise(rng,n,cutoff_freq=20):
    if n<=1: return zeros
    N=next_power_of_two(n)
    z=rng.standard_normal(N)
    F=np.fft.rfft(z)
    freqs=np.fft.rfftfreq(N)
    # smooth mask: 0.5*(1+tanh(5*(cutoff/freq-1)))? Use simple
    mask=np.ones_like(freqs)
    if cutoff_freq>0:
       mask = np.where(freqs < cutoff_freq, 1.0, 0.0)
    F*=mask
    out=np.fft.irfft(F,N=n)[:n]
    out=out-out.mean()
    sd=out.std()
    if sd>1e-15: out/=sd
    else: out=rng.standard_normal(n); out/=np.sqrt(n)
    return out
```
For n=10240, N=16384 maybe; noise length N then truncated; okay. Could use N=n next power. Good. Sharp mask may create discontinuities but noise smooth enough.

`perturb_sequence`:
```
def perturb_sequence(a,rng,strength=0.1):
    n=len(a); a=normalize_a(a)
    mode=int(rng.integers(0,6))
    if mode==0:
       noise=smooth_noise(rng,n, max(5,min(60,n//200+5)))
       q=a*np.exp(float(strength)*0.25*noise)
    elif mode==1:
       t=float(rng.uniform(0.02,0.12))
       q=(1-t)*a + t*(np.ones(n)/n)
    elif mode==2:
       noise=smooth_noise(...)
       q=a*np.exp(float(strength)*0.5*noise + float(rng.uniform(-0.05,0.05)))
    elif mode==3:
       # reverse/mirror blend
       t=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.3))
       q=(1-t)*a + t*a[::-1]
    elif mode==4:
       # local bump: choose center and width multiply
       c=float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.8)); w=max(0.02,float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.2)))
       x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
       bump=1+float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.8))*np.exp(-((x-c)/w)**2)
       q=a*bump
    else:
       # linear ramp perturb
       x=np.linspace(0,1,n); slope=float(rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5))
       q=a*np.exp(strength*slope*(2*x-1))
    return normalize_a(q)
```
This is top-level.

`add_candidate`:
```
def add_candidate(cands, arr, max_candidates=300):
    if len(cands)>=max_candidates: return
    arr=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size==0: return
    arr=np.nan_to_num(arr,nan=0.0,posinf=1e6,neginf=0.0)
    mn=float(np.min(arr))
    if mn<0: arr=arr-mn+1e-3
    if np.all(arr<=0): arr=np.ones_like(arr)
    a=normalize_a(arr)
    s=exact_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(s): cands.append((s,a))
```

`fourier_search` details:
```
def fourier_search(n, deadline, rng, parent):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    t=2*x-1
    B=np.column_stack((t, t*t*t, np.sin(2πx), np.cos(2πx), np.sin(4πx), np.cos(4πx), np.sin(6πx)))
    # normalize columns to unit L2 to make coeffs comparable
    norms=np.linalg.norm(B,axis=0,keepdims=True); norms[norms==0]=1; B=B/norms
    # starts
    starts=[]
    # zeros
    starts.append(np.zeros(B.shape[1]))
    # fit parent
    if parent is not None:
       par=resample_sequence(parent,n); y=np.log(np.maximum(par,1e-300)); y-=y.mean()
       try:
          theta=np.linalg.solve(B.T@B, B.T@y)
          theta=np.clip(theta,-2,2); starts.append(theta)
       except: pass
    # fixed linear/sine
    e=np.zeros(B.shape[1]); e[0]=1; starts.append(e)
    e[0]=-1; starts.append(e)
    e=np.zeros; e[2]=1; starts.append; e[2]=-1 ...
    # random
    for _ in range(4):
       th=rng.uniform(-1,1,size=B.shape[1]); starts.append(th)
    starts = starts[:10]
    best_s=inf; best_a=None
    for i,th in enumerate(starts):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       # evaluate raw
       a=make_from_b(th,B); if a is not None: sc=exact_score(a); update
       # local minimize with Powell but limited
       maxiter=200 if i<3 else 100
       try:
          res=minimize(fourier_func, th, args=(B,), method='Powell', options={'maxiter':maxiter,'xtol':1e-5,'ftol':1e-8})
       except Exception:
          res=x=th
       a=make_from_b(res.x,B); if a is not None: sc=exact_score(a); update
    return best_s,best_a
```
But `minimize` may ignore deadline and take seconds; okay. Could use Nelder-Mead faster? Powell maybe 200 cycles * D line searches? Might be too many. Pseudo code with exact variables:
```
def kkt_descent(a0, deadline, rng):
    a=normalize_a(a0)
    n=len(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_s=exact_score(a); s=best_s
    no_improve=0; outer=0
    k_list=(1,2,3,4,6,9,13,21)
    beta_list=(1.0,3.0,10.0,30.0,100.0,300.0,1000.0,3000.0)
    lin_factors=(0.005,0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0,10.0)
    mult_factors=(0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0)
    floor=1e-300
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       outer +=1
       d,S=d_from_a(a)
       A_corr,N_corr = pre_corr(a) if n < 30000 else None? pre for all if deadline time? Pre cost okay; do once.
       improved=False
       for mode_idx in range(len(k_list)+len(beta_list)):
          if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
          if mode_idx < len(k_list): k=k_list[mode_idx]; val=direct_corr_topk... C=dot; val=g-C
          else: beta=beta_list[mode_idx-len(k)]; p=softmax_weights; idx,wt,use_direct=...; if use_direct: g=direct... else: g=corr_dense_pre if A_corr is not None else corr_dense
          C=np.dot(a,g); val=g-C
          maxv=float(np.max(np.abs(val)))
          if maxv<1e-15: continue
          scale=float(np.max(a))/maxv
          if not finite or scale<=0: continue
          if scale>1e6: scale=1e6
          best_qs=s; best_q=a
          # linear
          for f in lin_factors:
             eta=scale*f
             q=a - eta*val
             if np.min(q)<0: q=np.clip(q,0,None)
             q=normalize_a(q)
             qs=exact_score(q)
             if finite and qs<best_qs: best_qs=qs; best_q=q
          # mult
          rel=val/np.maximum(a,floor)
          rel=np.clip(rel,-4.0,4.0)
          for f in mult_factors:
             q=a*np.exp(-f*rel)
             q=normalize_a(q)
             qs=exact_score(q)
             if finite and qs<best_qs: ...
          if best_qs < s - 1e-12:
             a=best_q; s=best_qs; improved=True
             if s < best_s: best_s=s; best_a=a.copy()
          elif no_improve > 12 and best_qs < s + 0.001 and best_qs < best_s + 0.004:
             a=best_q; s=best_qs
       if improved: no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve % 8 ==0:
             strength=min(0.25,0.04+0.01*no_improve)
             q=perturb_sequence(a,rng,strength)
             qs=exact_score(q)
             if qs < s + 0.002 and qs < best_s + 0.008:
                a=q; s=qs
             else:
                a=best_a.copy(); s=best_s
          if no_improve > 40:
             a=best_a.copy(); s=best_s; no_improve=0
       if outer % 20 ==0:
          print(label? we don't pass label; can print local)
    return best_s,best_a
```

`weights_to_direct_info`:
```
def weights_to_direct_info(p, threshold=1e-12, max_direct=80):
    idx=np.nonzero(p>threshold)[0]
    if idx.size==0:
       idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(p))]); wt=np.array([1.0]); return idx,wt,True
    wt=p[idx].copy()
    s=float(wt.sum())
    if s<=0: ...
    wt/=s
    if idx.size <= max_direct:
       return idx,wt,True
    return None,None,False
```

Now, `lse_path` and `fun_lseb` if included. Let's write it robustly. `fun_lseb` uses `weights_to_direct_info`? It needs `corr_dense` not pre. Use global. For p dense, corr_dense each eval. Good.

```
def fun_lseb(x,beta):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x=np.nan_to_num(x,nan=0,posinf=1e6,neginf=0); x=np.maximum(x,0)
    n=x.size; S=float(x.sum())
    if S<=1e-12: return 1e10, np.zeros(n)
    c=fast_self_conv(x); d=n*c/(S*S); d=np.maximum(d,0)
    dmax=float(np.max(d))
    if dmax<=0: return 0,zeros
    if beta <= 1e-6:
       p=np.ones(d.size)/d.size
    else:
       z=beta*(d-dmax); z=np.where(z<-50,-50,z); e=np.exp(z); ps=e.sum(); if ps<=0 onehot; p=e/ps
    L=dmax + (np.log(ps)/beta if beta>1e-6 else 0?) If beta small, LSE ~mean; compute logsum with z=0 ps=m; L=dmax+log(m)/0? no. For beta<=1e-6, just use max? Not use beta 0.
    idx,wt,use_direct=weights_to_direct_info(p)
    if use_direct: g=direct_corr_weights(x,idx,wt)
    else: g=corr_dense(x,p)
    C=np.dot(x,g)
    grad=(4.0*float(n)/(S**3))*(S*g - C)
    score=2.0*L + PEN_LSE*(S-1.0)**2
    grad += 2.0*PEN_LSE*(S-1.0)
    grad=np.nan_to_num(grad,nan=0,posinf=0,neginf=0)
    return score, grad
```
Need ps for beta small not. We can not use beta 0. L = dmax + logps/beta. If beta high and ps=1, ok. If underflow tail ignored, L approximate. Good.

`estimate_maxfun`:
```
def estimate_maxfun(left,n):
    left=max(5.0,float(left))
    base=0.003 * max(1.0, n/10000.0) * max(1.0, np.log2(max(16,n))/13.0)
    mf=int((0.8*left)/base)
    return max(80, min(800,mf))
```
Assume 3ms per eval. 2400s -> 800*? cap okay.

`lse_path`:
```
def lse_path(a_start, deadline):
   a=normalize_a(a_start); best=a; best_s=exact_score(a); s=best_s
   betas=(20.0,100.0,500.0,2000.0,10000.0,50000.0)
   for beta in betas:
      while time.monotonic() < deadline:
         left=deadline-time
         mf=estimate_maxfun(left,n)
         if mf<80: break
         sc,a2=run_lbfgs(a,beta,deadline,mf)
         sc2=exact_score(a2)
         if finite and sc2<best_s: best_s=sc2; best=a2.copy()
         if finite and sc2 < best_s*1.05 + 1e-9:
            a=normalize_a(a2)
         else:
            a=best.copy()
         # If LSE score not improving? We don't track. Could if sc >= s? no.
         if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10: break
   return best_s,best
```
But if LSE exact worsens repeatedly, reset. Good.

`try_other_lengths`:
```
def try_other_lengths(best_pair, deadline, rng, n_list):
    best_s,best_a=best_pair
    remaining=len(n_list)
    for i,n in enumerate(n_list):
       if time>deadline: break
       left=deadline-time
       chunk=max(60.0, left/(remaining-i)) - 2
       end=time.monotonic()+min(chunk,left)
       a_start=resample_sequence(best_a,n)
       a,s=kkt_descent(a_start,end,rng)
       if s<best_s: best_s=s; best_a=a
    return best_s,best_a
```
If n differs, subsequent updates use new best. Good.

In `propose_candidate`, after multi-length if best_a length changed, final polish on that length. Good.

def finalize_sequence(best_a, deadline):
   # one final small kkt
   if time<deadline-20:
      a,s=kkt_descent(best_a, deadline-5, rng)
   a=normalize_a(a)
   # final check no inf
   out=[float(v) for v in a if np.isfinite(v)]
   # ensure nonnegative; clip
   out=[max(0.0,min(1000.0,x)) for x in out]
   S=sum(out)
   if S<0.01: out=[0.1]*1024? but length? Use n.
   else: out=[x/(S)*1.0 for x in out]
   # If verified inf? call evaluate
   try:
       vs=evaluate_sequence(out)
       print...
   except: pass
   return out
```

best_s,best_a=kkt_descent(best_a,t_final,rng)

def load_parent_sequence():
    try:
       h=globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    except Exception: h=None
    if h is None: return None
    try:
       p=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       p=np.nan_to_num(p,nan=0,posinf=1e6,neginf=0)
       p=np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
       if p.size<8: return None
       return p
    except: return None
```

def choose_length(parent):
   if parent is not None:
      n=parent.size
      if n >= 4096 and n <= 16384: return int(n)
      if n > 16384: return 16384
      if n < 4096: return 10240
   return 10240
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2.

Main ideas:
- FFT-based exact scoring and correlation directions.
- A KKT-style fixed-point/line-search optimizer for active convolution lags.
  For normalized a, the projected gradient of a weighted active-lag surrogate is
  g - C, where g = sum_k w_k a_{k-i} and C = dot(a, g).  We line-search both
  linear and multiplicative steps in this direction and accept exact improvements.
- Low-dimensional Fourier/shape search for better starts.
- Optional L-BFGS-B on a smoothed log-sum-exp surrogate, kept safe and time-limited.
- Restart/perturbation passes and optional multi-length exploration.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None

PEN_LSE = 1e-6
DIRECT_MAX_NNZ = 80


def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(M)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:M]
    return np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def normalize_a(a, target=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, target / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (float(target) / s)


def d_from_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64), s
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    d = (float(n) * c) / (s * s)
    return np.maximum(d, 0.0), s


def exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    d, s = d_from_a(a)
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    if d.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(2.0 * np.max(d))


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    m = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a).astype(np.float64)


def direct_corr_weights(a, idx, weights):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0:
        return out
    idx = np.asarray(idx, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    weights = np.asarray(weights, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = min(idx.size, weights.size)
    for ii in range(m):
        kk = int(idx[ii])
        ww = float(weights[ii])
        if ww == 0.0 or kk < 0 or kk >= 2 * n - 1:
            continue
        i0 = kk - (n - 1)
        if i0 < 0:
            i0 = 0
        i1 = kk
        if i1 >= n:
            i1 = n - 1
        if i0 > i1:
            continue
        out[i0:i1 + 1] += ww * a[kk - i1:kk - i0 + 1][::-1]
    return out


def topk_indices(d, k):
    m = int(d.size)
    k = int(max(1, min(k, m)))
    if k == 1:
        return np.array([int(np.argmax(d))], dtype=np.int64)
    if k >= m:
        return np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
    return np.argpartition(d, m - k)[m - k:].astype(np.int64)


def direct_corr_topk(a, d, k):
    n = int(a.size)
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0 or d.size == 0:
        return out
    idx = topk_indices(d, k)
    inv = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
    for kk in idx:
        kk = int(kk)
        i0 = kk - (n - 1)
        if i0 < 0:
            i0 = 0
        i1 = kk
        if i1 >= n:
            i1 = n - 1
        if i0 > i1:
            continue
        out[i0:i1 + 1] += inv * a[kk - i1:kk - i0 + 1][::-1]
    return out


def corr_dense(a, w):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(w.size)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    total = n + m - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(total)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    full = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)[:total]
    return full[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def pre_corr(a):
    n = int(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).size)
    N = next_power_of_two(3 * n - 2)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    return A, N


def corr_dense_pre(a, A, w):
    n = int(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).size)
    if n == 0 or A is None:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    N = int(A.size)
    M = 3 * n - 2
    W = np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), N)
    full = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)[:M]
    return full[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def softmax_weights(d, beta):
    m = int(np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).size)
    if m == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    z = beta * (d - dmax)
    z = np.where(z < -50.0, -50.0, z)
    e = np.exp(z)
    ps = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ps) or ps <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(d))] = 1.0
        return w
    return e / ps


def weights_to_direct_info(p, threshold=1e-12, max_direct=DIRECT_MAX_NNZ):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(p.size)
    if m == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.int64), np.array([], dtype=np.float64), False
    idx = np.nonzero(p > threshold)[0].astype(np.int64)
    if idx.size == 0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(p))], dtype=np.int64)
        wt = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        return idx, wt, True
    wt = p[idx].astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    s = float(wt.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(p))], dtype=np.int64)
        wt = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        return idx, wt, True
    wt /= s
    if idx.size <= max_direct:
        return idx, wt, True
    return idx, wt, False


def smooth_noise(rng, n, cutoff_freq=20.0):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    N = next_power_of_two(n)
    z = rng.standard_normal(N)
    F = np.fft.rfft(z)
    freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(N)
    cutoff = float(max(2.0, cutoff_freq))
    mask = np.ones_like(freqs, dtype=np.float64)
    mask[freqs >= cutoff] = 0.0
    F *= mask
    out = np.fft.irfft(F, N=n)[:n]
    out = out - float(out.mean())
    sd = float(out.std())
    if sd > 1e-15:
        out = out / sd
    else:
        out = rng.standard_normal(n)
        out = (out - float(out.mean())) / max(1e-15, float(out.std()))
    return out


def perturb_sequence(a, rng, strength=0.1):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 6))
    cutoff = max(4.0, min(80.0, float(n) / 120.0))

    if mode == 0:
        noise = smooth_noise(rng, n, cutoff)
        q = a * np.exp(float(strength) * 0.25 * noise)
    elif mode == 1:
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.12))
        q = (1.0 - t) * a + t * (np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
    elif mode == 2:
        noise = smooth_noise(rng, n, cutoff * 0.5)
        q = a * np.exp(float(strength) * 0.6 * noise)
    elif mode == 3:
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35))
        q = (1.0 - t) * a + t * a[::-1]
    elif mode == 4:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        c = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.85))
        w = float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.18))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.0))
        q = a * (1.0 + amp * np.exp(-((x - c) / w) ** 2))
    else:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        slope = float(rng.uniform(-0.8, 0.8))
        q = a * np.exp(float(strength) * slope * (2.0 * x - 1.0))

    return normalize_a(q)


def add_candidate(cands, arr, n, max_candidates=220):
    if len(cands) >= max_candidates:
        return
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size != int(n):
        arr = resample_sequence(arr, int(n))
    if arr.size != int(n):
        arr = np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    mn = float(np.min(arr))
    if mn < 0.0:
        arr = arr - mn + 1e-3
    if float(np.max(arr)) <= 0.0:
        arr = np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    a = normalize_a(arr)
    sc = exact_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        cands.append((float(sc), a))


def generate_initial_candidates(n, parent, rng):
    n = int(n)
    cands = []
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)

    if parent is not None:
        add_candidate(cands, parent, n)
        add_candidate(cands, parent[::-1], n)

    add_candidate(cands, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n)

    for alpha in (-0.9, -0.6, -0.3, -0.15, 0.15, 0.3, 0.6, 0.9):
        add_candidate(cands, 1.0 + alpha * (2.0 * x - 1.0), n)

    for r in (-3.0, -2.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
        add_candidate(cands, np.exp(r * x), n)

    for a1 in (-1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0):
        for a2 in (-0.5, 0.0, 0.5):
            base = (
                1.0
                + a1 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
                + a2 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x)
                + 0.35 * a2 * np.sin(6.0 * np.pi * x)
            )
            add_candidate(cands, base, n)

    for phi in (0.0, 0.5 * np.pi, np.pi, 1.5 * np.pi):
        add_candidate(cands, 1.0 + 0.7 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x + phi), n)

    for p in (0.25, 0.35, 0.5, 0.65, 0.75):
        for hi, lo in ((2.0, 0.25), (1.6, 0.5), (1.3, 0.7), (1.1, 0.9)):
            add_candidate(cands, np.where(x < p, hi, lo), n)

    for _ in range(12):
        noise = smooth_noise(rng, n, max(5.0, min(60.0, float(n) / 200.0)))
        add_candidate(cands, 1.0 + 0.6 * noise, n)

    if not cands:
        a = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        return exact_score(a), a

    best_s, best_a = min(cands, key=lambda z: z[0])
    return float(best_s), best_a


def make_from_b(theta, B):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    B = np.asarray(B, dtype=np.float64)
    v = B.dot(theta)
    v = np.clip(v, -2.75, 2.75)
    a = np.exp(v)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s


def fourier_func(theta, B):
    a = make_from_b(theta, B)
    if a is None:
        return 1e10
    return exact_score(a)


def fit_bcoeffs(a, B):
    y = np.log(np.maximum(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), 1e-300))
    y = y - float(y.mean())
    A = B.T.dot(B)
    b = B.T.dot(y)
    try:
        th = np.linalg.solve(A, b)
    except Exception:
        try:
            th = np.linalg.lstsq(A, b, rcond=None)[0]
        except Exception:
            th = np.zeros(B.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
    return np.clip(np.asarray(th, dtype=np.float64), -2.25, 2.25)


def fourier_search(n, deadline, rng, parent):
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return np.inf, None
    n = int(n)
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    t = 2.0 * x - 1.0

    B = np.column_stack((
        t,
        t * t * t,
        np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x),
        np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x),
        np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x),
        np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x),
        np.sin(6.0 * np.pi * x),
    ))

    norms = np.linalg.norm(B, axis=0, keepdims=True)
    norms[norms == 0.0] = 1.0
    B = B / norms

    starts = []
    d = int(B.shape[1])

    e = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
    starts.append(e.copy())

    if parent is not None:
        try:
            par = resample_sequence(parent, n)
            starts.append(fit_bcoeffs(par, B))
        except Exception:
            pass

    e = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64); e[0] = 1.0; starts.append(e.copy())
    e = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64); e[0] = -1.0; starts.append(e.copy())
    e = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64); e[2] = 1.0; starts.append(e.copy())
    e = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64); e[2] = -0.7; starts.append(e.copy())
    e = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64); e[3] = 0.6; starts.append(e.copy())

    for _ in range(5):
        starts.append(rng.uniform(-1.2, 1.2, size=d).astype(np.float64))

    starts = starts[:12]
    best_s = np.inf
    best_a = None

    for i, th in enumerate(starts):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        a = make_from_b(th, B)
        if a is not None:
            sc = exact_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s:
                best_s = float(sc)
                best_a = a.copy()

        if _scipy_minimize is not None and (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 15.0:
            maxiter = 180 if i < 4 else 90
            try:
                res = _scipy_minimize(
                    fourier_func,
                    np.asarray(th, dtype=np.float64),
                    args=(B,),
                    method="Nelder-Mead",
                    options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "xatol": 1e-5, "fatol": 1e-9},
                )
                if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                    a2 = make_from_b(res.x, B)
                    if a2 is not None:
                        sc2 = exact_score(a2)
                        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_s:
                            best_s = float(sc2)
                            best_a = a2.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    return float(best_s) if np.isfinite(best_s) else np.inf, best_a


def fun_lseb(x, beta):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    n = int(x.size)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if n == 0 or s <= 1e-12:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c = fast_self_conv(x)
    d = (float(n) * c) / (s * s)
    d = np.maximum(d, 0.0)
    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    if dmax <= 0.0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 1e-3:
        p = np.ones(d.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(d.size)
        ps = float(d.size)
    else:
        z = beta * (d - dmax)
        z = np.where(z < -50.0, -50.0, z)
        e = np.exp(z)
        ps = float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ps) or ps <= 0.0:
            p = np.zeros(d.size, dtype=np.float64)
            p[int(np.argmax(d))] = 1.0
        else:
            p = e / ps

    L = dmax + (np.log(max(ps, 1e-300)) / beta if beta > 1e-3 else 0.0)

    idx, wt, use_direct = weights_to_direct_info(p)
    if use_direct:
        g = direct_corr_weights(x, idx, wt)
    else:
        g = corr_dense(x, p)

    C = float(np.dot(x, g))
    grad = (4.0 * float(n) / (s ** 3)) * (s * g - C)
    score = 2.0 * L + PEN_LSE * (s - 1.0) ** 2
    grad = grad + 2.0 * PEN_LSE * (s - 1.0)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return float(score), grad


def estimate_maxfun(left, n):
    left = max(10.0, float(left))
    n = max(16, int(n))
    est = 0.003 * max(1.0, float(n) / 10000.0) * max(1.0, float(np.log2(max(16, n))) / 13.0)
    mf = int((0.8 * left) / max(est, 1e-6))
    return max(80, min(900, mf))


def run_lbfgs(a0, beta, deadline, maxfun):
    a = normalize_a(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if _scipy_minimize is None:
        return exact_score(a), a
    if (deadline - time.monotonic()) < 15.0 or n == 0:
        return exact_score(a), a

    maxfun = int(max(80, maxfun))
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    opts = {
        "ftol": 1e-15,
        "gtol": 1e-12,
        "maxfun": maxfun,
        "maxiter": max(40, maxfun // 2),
        "maxls": 30,
    }
    try:
        res = _scipy_minimize(fun_lseb, a, method="L-BFGS-B", jac=True,
                              args=(float(beta),), bounds=bounds, options=opts)
    except Exception:
        return exact_score(a), a

    a2 = normalize_a(res.x) if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None else a
    sc = exact_score(a2)
    if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc > 1e7:
        return exact_score(a), a
    return float(sc), a2


def lse_path(a_start, deadline):
    if _scipy_minimize is None:
        a = normalize_a(a_start)
        return exact_score(a), a
    a = normalize_a(a_start)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_s = exact_score(a)
    betas = (20.0, 100.0, 500.0, 2000.0, 8000.0, 30000.0)

    for beta in betas:
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            left = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
            if left < 20.0:
                break
            mf = estimate_maxfun(left, len(a))
            if mf < 80:
                break
            sc, a2 = run_lbfgs(a, beta, deadline, mf)
            sc2 = exact_score(a2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_s:
                best_s = float(sc2)
                best_a = a2.copy()
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_s * 1.05 + 1e-8:
                a = normalize_a(a2)
            else:
                a = best_a.copy()
            if (deadline - time.monotonic()) < 20.0:
                break
    return float(best_s), best_a


def kkt_descent(a0, deadline, rng):
    a = normalize_a(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, a

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_s = exact_score(a)
    s = float(best_s)
    outer = 0
    no_improve = 0

    k_list = (1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 13, 21)
    beta_list = (1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0, 100.0, 300.0, 1000.0, 3000.0)
    lin_factors = (0.003, 0.01, 0.03, 0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0)
    mult_factors = (0.01, 0.03, 0.1, 0.3, 1.0)
    floor = 1e-300

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        outer += 1
        d, _ = d_from_a(a)
        if d.size == 0:
            break

        A_corr, _ = pre_corr(a)
        outer_best_qs = float(s)
        outer_best_q = a

        for mode_idx in range(len(k_list) + len(beta_list)):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break

            if mode_idx < len(k_list):
                k = int(k_list[mode_idx])
                g = direct_corr_topk(a, d, k)
            else:
                beta = float(beta_list[mode_idx - len(k_list)])
                p = softmax_weights(d, beta)
                idx, wt, use_direct = weights_to_direct_info(p)
                if use_direct:
                    g = direct_corr_weights(a, idx, wt)
                else:
                    g = corr_dense_pre(a, A_corr, p)

            C = float(np.dot(a, g))
            val = g - C
            maxv = float(np.max(np.abs(val)))
            if not np.isfinite(maxv) or maxv <= 1e-15:
                continue

            scale = float(np.max(a)) / maxv
            if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 0.0:
                continue
            if scale > 1e6:
                scale = 1e6

            dir_best_qs = float(s)
            dir_best_q = a

            for f in lin_factors:
                eta = scale * float(f)
                q = a - eta * val
                if np.min(q) < 0.0:
                    q = np.clip(q, 0.0, None)
                q = normalize_a(q)
                qs = exact_score(q)
                if np.isfinite(qs) and qs < dir_best_qs:
                    dir_best_qs = float(qs)
                    dir_best_q = q

            rel = val / np.maximum(a, floor)
            rel = np.clip(rel, -4.0, 4.0)
            for f in mult_factors:
                q = a * np.exp(-float(f) * rel)
                q = normalize_a(q)
                qs = exact_score(q)
                if np.isfinite(qs) and qs < dir_best_qs:
                    dir_best_qs = float(qs)
                    dir_best_q = q

            if np.isfinite(dir_best_qs) and dir_best_qs < outer_best_qs:
                outer_best_qs = float(dir_best_qs)
                outer_best_q = dir_best_q

        if np.isfinite(outer_best_qs) and outer_best_qs < s - 1e-12:
            a = outer_best_q
            s = float(outer_best_qs)
            no_improve = 0
            if s < best_s:
                best_s = float(s)
                best_a = a.copy()
                if outer % 25 == 0:
                    print("KKT improved new best {:.9f}".format(best_s))
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve % 8 == 0:
                strength = min(0.28, 0.05 + 0.012 * float(no_improve))
                q = perturb_sequence(a, rng, strength)
                qs = exact_score(q)
                if np.isfinite(qs) and qs < s + 0.002 and qs < best_s + 0.008:
                    a = q
                    s = float(qs)
                else:
                    a = best_a.copy()
                    s = float(best_s)
            if no_improve > 45:
                a = best_a.copy()
                s = float(best_s)
                no_improve = 0

        if outer % 50 == 0:
            print("KKT outer {} current {:.9f} best {:.9f}".format(outer, s, best_s))

    return float(best_s), best_a


def try_other_lengths(best_score, best_a, deadline, rng, n_list):
    for n in n_list:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        n = int(n)
        if n <= 0:
            continue
        a = resample_sequence(best_a, n)
        sc, a2 = kkt_descent(a, deadline, rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_a = a2
            print("Other length {}: {:.9f}".format(n, best_score))
    return best_score, best_a


def finalize_sequence(a, best_score, rng, deadline):
    if (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 60.0:
        try:
            sc, a = kkt_descent(a, deadline - 5.0, rng)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc)
        except Exception:
            pass

    a = normalize_a(a)
    out = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        out = np.full(max(64, out.size), 1.0 / max(64, out.size), dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(out.sum())
    else:
        out = out / s
    return [float(v) for v in out], float(best_score)


def load_parent_sequence():
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    except Exception:
        h = None
    if h is None:
        return None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size < 8:
            return None
        return p
    except Exception:
        return None


def choose_length(parent):
    if parent is not None:
        n = int(parent.size)
        if 4096 <= n <= 16384:
            return n
        if n > 16384:
            return 16384
    return 10240


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = float(max(600.0, budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + total - 45.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    parent = load_parent_sequence()
    n = choose_length(parent)

    print("Starting search: n={}, parent={}".format(n, parent is not None))

    best_score, best_seq = generate_initial_candidates(n, parent, rng)
    print("Initial best: {:.9f}".format(best_score))

    # Low-dimensional shape search for a better start.
    t_low = t0 + 0.03 * total
    try:
        sc, seq = fourier_search(n, t_low, rng, parent)
        if sc is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_seq = seq
            print("Fourier search best: {:.9f}".format(best_score))
    except Exception as e:
        print("Fourier search skipped: {}".format(e))

    # Main KKT descent.
    t1 = t0 + 0.45 * total
    try:
        sc, seq = kkt_descent(best_seq, t1, rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_seq = seq
            print("Main KKT best: {:.9f}".format(best_score))
    except Exception as e:
        print("Main KKT error: {}".format(e))

    # Smooth LSE / L-BFGS-B polish.
    t2 = t0 + 0.60 * total
    if _scipy_minimize is not None and (t2 - time.monotonic()) > 60.0:
        try:
            sc, seq = lse_path(best_seq, t2)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_seq = seq
                print("LSE L-BFGS best: {:.9f}".format(best_score))
        except Exception as e:
            print("LSE L-BFGS error: {}".format(e))
    else:
        # Use some extra time for another KKT pass if SciPy is unavailable.
        try:
            sc, seq = kkt_descent(best_seq, min(t2, deadline - 20.0), rng)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_seq = seq
                print("Extra KKT best: {:.9f}".format(best_score))
        except Exception as e:
            print("Extra KKT error: {}".format(e))

    # KKT polynomial continuation/refinement.
    t3 = t0 + 0.80 * total
    try:
        sc, seq = kkt_descent(best_seq, t3, rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_seq = seq
            print("Second KKT best: {:.9f}".format(best_score))
    except Exception as e:
        print("Second KKT error: {}".format(e))

    # A few perturbed restarts from the current best.
    t4 = t0 + 0.89 * total
    remaining_trials = 3
    for _ in range(remaining_trials):
        if time.monotonic() >= t4:
            break
        left = float(t4 - time.monotonic())
        if left <= 30.0:
            break
        chunk = max(25.0, left / float(remaining_trials)) - 5.0
        end = min(t4, time.monotonic() + chunk)
        remaining_trials -= 1
        q = perturb_sequence(best_seq, rng, strength=float(rng.uniform(0.08, 0.25)))
        qs = exact_score(q)
        if not np.isfinite(qs) or qs > best_score * 1.005 + 1e-8:
            q = best_seq.copy()
        try:
            sc, seq = kkt_descent(q, end, rng)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_seq = seq
                print("Restart best: {:.9f}".format(best_score))
        except Exception as e:
            print("Restart error: {}".format(e))

    # Multi-length exploration.
    t5 = t0 + 0.95 * total
    other_lengths = []
    if n != 8192:
        other_lengths.append(8192)
    if n != 16384:
        other_lengths.append(16384)
    if other_lengths and (t5 - time.monotonic()) > 80.0:
        try:
            best_score, best_seq = try_other_lengths(best_score, best_seq, t5, rng, other_lengths[:2])
        except Exception as e:
            print("Multi-length error: {}".format(e))

    # Final polish.
    try:
        final_seq, final_score = finalize_sequence(best_seq, best_score, rng, deadline)
    except Exception:
        final_seq = [float(v) for v in normalize_a(best_seq)]
        final_score = float(best_score)

    print("FINAL internal={:.9f} length={} time={:.1f}s".format(
        final_score, len(final_seq), time.monotonic() - t0))
    return final_seq
```
